# libcrypto-3-x64.dll - unified dossier

**GMS v270**: 4,596,312 bytes, linked 2024-02-06, 178 imported / 5376 exported.
**MSEA**: not shipped in the audited MSEA corpus.
**Status**: GMS-only.

## MSEA build

Not shipped in the audited MSEA corpus - GMS-only module.

## GMS v270 dossier

Role and version: OpenSSL cryptographic primitives and support library, version `3.0.13` (PE FileVersion/ProductVersion and canonical embedded string `OpenSSL 3.0.13`). `libssl-3-x64.dll` and `libcurl.dll` import it; the game EXE imports libcurl, so it is part of the client's indirect TLS/crypto dependency chain.

Exports: 5,376 total. Largest symbol families: EVP 859, X509 590, OSSL 469, ASN1 231, i2d 219, d2i 215, BN 196, BIO 179, TS 151, EC 149, OCSP 139, PEM 135, CMS 125, ENGINE 121, RSA 118, and 1,480 in the remaining families.

Notable imports and strings: `libssl-3-x64.dll` imports 491 crypto symbols; `libcurl.dll` imports 162, including `OpenSSL_version`, EVP digest, X509/PEM, BIO, and certificate routines. The extracted version string is `OpenSSL 3.0.13`.

Integration notes: The EXE's `curl_easy_init` import, libcurl's OpenSSL imports, and libssl's crypto imports establish the static dependency chain. `analysis/engine-delta.json` records libcurl additions of 162 crypto symbols. This identifies the TLS backend surface but not specific application data or runtime use.

Evidence: `analysis/pe_inventory.json`; `analysis/engine-delta.json`; `sections/_tables/libcrypto-3-x64.imports-exports.md`; `sections/_tables/libssl-3-x64.imports-exports.md`; `sections/_tables/libcurl.imports-exports.md`; `strings/libcrypto-3-x64.strings.txt`; PE version resource at `<client-root>/libcrypto-3-x64.dll`.
